, it’s obvious

\displaystyle\left|E_{7}\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha}\|\partial_{y}^{2}b\|_{L_{\mathrm{v}}^{\infty}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|\sqrt{a}\mathcal{U}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}\tilde{\zeta}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{23}{3},0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\langle t\rangle^{2\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{4}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\mathcal{U}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}\tilde{\zeta}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{23}{3},0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+C\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\tilde{\zeta}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{23}{3},0}},

here we use the fact that
\alpha\leq\frac{1}{2}\gamma_{0}+\frac{5}{8}.

Combining
E_{1}-E_{7}
, we can deduce that when
\alpha\leq\min\{\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4},\frac{1}{2}\gamma_{0}+\frac{5}{8}\}
,
F_{4}
satisfies

\displaystyle\left|a\left(\left[\frac{1}{\dot{\theta}}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}b};\mathcal{L_{\kappa}}\right]\int_{y}^{\infty}\mathcal{U}dz,\tilde{\zeta}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\right|\leq C(1+\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda)\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+C(1+\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda)\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\tilde{\zeta}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{23}{3},0}}.

For
F_{5}
, referring to the estimates of
F_{3}
, we can gain

\displaystyle\left|a\left(\frac{2(1-\kappa)}{3\dot{\theta}}\delta(t)T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}D_{x}b}Q(D_{x})\partial_{y}\mathcal{U},\tilde{\zeta}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\right|\ \leq C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\tilde{\zeta}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{23}{3},0}}+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}.

For
F_{6}
, similar to
F_{4}
, we obtain by direct calculation

\displaystyle\left[\frac{2\delta(t)}{3\dot{\theta}}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}D_{x}b}Q(D_{x});\mathcal{L_{\kappa}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{3}\partial_{t}\left(\frac{\delta(t)}{\dot{\theta}}\right)T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}D_{x}b}Q(D_{x})-\frac{2\delta(t)}{3\dot{\theta}}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{t}\partial_{y}D_{x}b-\kappa\partial_{y}^{3}D_{x}b}Q(D_{x})

\displaystyle+\frac{2\delta(t)}{3}\lambda\left[T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}D_{x}b}Q(D_{x});[D_{x}]^{\frac{2}{3}}\right]+\frac{2\delta(t)}{3\dot{\theta}}\left[T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}D_{x}b}Q(D_{x});T^{\mathrm{h}}_{u}\partial_{x}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{2\delta(t)}{3\dot{\theta}}\left[T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}D_{x}b}Q(D_{x});T^{\mathrm{h}}_{v}\partial_{y}\right]+\frac{4\delta^{2}(t)}{9\dot{\theta}}\left[T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}D_{x}b}Q(D_{x});T^{\mathrm{h}}_{D_{x}u}Q(D_{x})\partial_{x}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{4\kappa\delta(t)}{3\dot{\theta}}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}^{2}D_{x}b}Q(D_{x})\partial_{y}.

Noticing (
2.12
) and (
3.14
), we derive

0<\delta(t)\leq\delta<C,

\partial_{t}\left(\frac{\delta(t)}{\dot{\theta}}\right)=-\lambda+\delta(t)\alpha\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-1}.

So similar to the estimates of
F_{4}
, we can obtain the estimates of
F_{6}

\displaystyle~~~~~\left|a\left(\left[\frac{2\delta(t)}{3\dot{\theta}}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}D_{x}b}Q(D_{x});\mathcal{L_{\kappa}}\right]\int_{y}^{\infty}\mathcal{U}dz,\tilde{\zeta}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq C(1+\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda)\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+C(1+\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda)\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\tilde{\zeta}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{23}{3},0}}.

Now start computing the left side of (
5.5
), similar to (
4.4
), we can get

(5.8)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\left(\partial_{t}\tilde{\zeta}-\kappa\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{\zeta},\tilde{\zeta}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\geq\frac{1}{2}\dfrac{d}{dt}\|\tilde{\zeta}(t)\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+2l_{\kappa}\|\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta}(t)\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}.\end{split}

Then

(5.9)

\displaystyle\begin{split}a(\mathcal{L_{\kappa}}\tilde{\zeta},\tilde{\zeta})_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}&\geq\frac{1}{2}\dfrac{d}{dt}\|\sqrt{a}\tilde{\zeta}(t)\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}-\frac{1}{2}\|\sqrt{a^{\prime}}\tilde{\zeta}(t)\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\lambda\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\tilde{\zeta}(t)\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\\
&+2l_{\kappa}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta}(t)\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+a\left(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{u}\partial_{x}\tilde{\zeta},\tilde{\zeta}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+a\left(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{v}\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta},\tilde{\zeta}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\\
&+a\left(\frac{2}{3}\delta(t)T^{\mathrm{h}}_{D_{x}u}Q(D_{x})\partial_{x}\tilde{\zeta},\tilde{\zeta}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}.\end{split}

Using the same method of estimates as
a\left(\mathcal{L}\mathcal{U},\mathcal{U}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}
, we can deduce

\left|a\left(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{u}\partial_{x}\tilde{\zeta},\tilde{\zeta}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\right|\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\tilde{\zeta}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{23}{3},0}},

\left|a\left(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{v}\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta},\tilde{\zeta}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\right|\leq C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\tilde{\zeta}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{23}{3},0}}+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}},

\left|a\left(\frac{2}{3}\delta(t)T^{\mathrm{h}}_{D_{x}u}Q(D_{x})\partial_{x}\tilde{\zeta},\tilde{\zeta}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\right|\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\tilde{\zeta}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{23}{3},0}}.

Combining all the above estimates and integrating over
[0,t]
, noting that (
5.3
), we can obtain the following estimates

(5.10)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &~~~~~\|\sqrt{a}\tilde{\zeta}(t)\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}-\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a^{\prime}}\tilde{\zeta}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}ds+(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{1}{50}\eta)\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}ds\\
&~~~~~+2\left(\lambda-C(1+\lambda\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\right)\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\tilde{\zeta}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{23}{3},0}}ds\\
&\leq\|\sqrt{a}(0)b_{\Phi}(0)\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\frac{1}{25}\eta\int_{0}^{t}\left(\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}u_{\Phi},\partial_{y}b_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}\right)ds\\
&~~~~~+C(1+\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda)\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\left(\|\sqrt{a}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+\|\sqrt{a}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\right)ds.\end{aligned}

Finally we come back to give the estimates of

\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}u_{\Phi},\partial_{y}b_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

and
\|\sqrt{a}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}
.

By (
3.13
), we have

(5.11)

\displaystyle\begin{split}u_{\Phi}=\zeta+\frac{1}{\dot{\theta}}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}\int_{y}^{\infty}\mathcal{U}dz+\frac{2\delta(t)}{3\dot{\theta}}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}D_{x}u}Q(D_{x})\int_{y}^{\infty}\mathcal{U}dz,\\
b_{\Phi}=\tilde{\zeta}+\frac{1}{\dot{\theta}}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}b}\int_{y}^{\infty}\mathcal{U}dz+\frac{2\delta(t)}{3\dot{\theta}}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}D_{x}b}Q(D_{x})\int_{y}^{\infty}\mathcal{U}dz.\end{split}

Using (
3.14
), (
4.3
), lemma
2.2
and lemma
4.1
, it yields

(5.12)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\|\frac{1}{\dot{\theta}}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}\int_{y}^{\infty}\mathcal{U}dz\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\mathcal{U}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\mathcal{U}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}},\\
&\|\frac{2\delta(t)}{3\dot{\theta}}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}D_{x}u}Q(D_{x})\int_{y}^{\infty}\mathcal{U}dz\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\mathcal{U}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\mathcal{U}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}},\\
&\|\frac{1}{\dot{\theta}}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}b}\int_{y}^{\infty}\mathcal{U}dz\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\mathcal{U}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\mathcal{U}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}},\\
&\|\frac{2\delta(t)}{3\dot{\theta}}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}D_{x}b}Q(D_{x})\int_{y}^{\infty}\mathcal{U}dz\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\mathcal{U}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\mathcal{U}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}.\end{aligned}

Combing (
5.11
) and (
5.12
), we know

(5.13)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\sqrt{a}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}&\leq\|\sqrt{a}(\zeta,\tilde{\zeta})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\sqrt{a}\mathcal{U}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\\
&\leq\|\sqrt{a}(\zeta,\tilde{\zeta})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{23}{3},0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\sqrt{a}\mathcal{U}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}.\end{split}

With
\partial_{y}
acting on (
5.11
), we can get

(5.14)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\partial_{y}u_{\Phi}&=\partial_{y}\zeta+\frac{1}{\dot{\theta}}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}^{2}u}\int_{y}^{\infty}\mathcal{U}dz+\frac{2\delta(t)}{3\dot{\theta}}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}^{2}D_{x}u}Q(D_{x})\int_{y}^{\infty}\mathcal{U}dz\\
&-\frac{1}{\dot{\theta}}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}\mathcal{U}-\frac{2\delta(t)}{3\dot{\theta}}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}D_{x}u}Q(D_{x})\mathcal{U},\\
\partial_{y}b_{\Phi}&=\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta}+\frac{1}{\dot{\theta}}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}^{2}b}\int_{y}^{\infty}\mathcal{U}dz+\frac{2\delta(t)}{3\dot{\theta}}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}^{2}D_{x}b}Q(D_{x})\int_{y}^{\infty}\mathcal{U}dz\\
&-\frac{1}{\dot{\theta}}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}b}\mathcal{U}-\frac{2\delta(t)}{3\dot{\theta}}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}D_{x}b}Q(D_{x})\mathcal{U}.\end{split}

Similarly by (
3.14
), (
4.3
), lemma
2.2
and
lemma
4.1
, we can deduce

(5.15)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}u_{\Phi},\partial_{y}b_{\Phi})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}\leq\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\sqrt{a}\mathcal{U}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}.\end{split}

At this time using lemma
2.7
, we can obtain

(5.16)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}u_{\Phi},\partial_{y}b_{\Phi})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}\leq\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}.\end{split}

Next, choose
\epsilon_{*}
sufficiently small so that when
\epsilon<\epsilon_{*}
,
C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}<1
holds. At this point, combining (
5.10
), (
5.13
) and (
5.16
),
we can get

(5.17)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &~~~~~\|\sqrt{a}\tilde{\zeta}(t)\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}-\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a^{\prime}}\tilde{\zeta}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}ds+(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{1}{50}\eta)\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}ds\\
&~~~~~+2\left(\lambda-C(1+\lambda\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\right)\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\tilde{\zeta}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{23}{3},0}}ds\\
&\leq\|\sqrt{a}(0)(u_{\Phi}(0),b_{\Phi}(0))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\frac{2}{25}\eta\int_{0}^{t}\left(\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\right)ds\\
&~~~~~+C(1+\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda)\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\left(\|\sqrt{a}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\|\sqrt{a}(P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+\|\sqrt{a}(\zeta,\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{23}{3},0}}\right)ds.\end{aligned}

This completes the estimates of
\tilde{\zeta}
, and the estimates of
\zeta
follow in the same way. After sorting out the coefficients, we complete the proof of proposition
5.1
.
∎

Applying proposition
5.1
, we can prove proposition
3.2
.

Proof of Proposition
3.2
:

Take
a(t)=\langle t\rangle^{2l_{\kappa}-2\eta}
in (
5.4
). Then by lemma
2.7
we can get

\displaystyle-(2l_{\kappa}-2\eta)\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta-\frac{1}{2}}(\zeta,\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}ds+(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{1}{10}\eta)\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle-(4l_{\kappa}-4\eta)\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}ds+(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{1}{10}\eta)\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\eta\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}ds,

thus we have

\displaystyle~~~~~\|\langle t\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\zeta(t),\tilde{\zeta}(t))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\eta\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle~~~~~+2\left(\lambda-C(1+\lambda\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\right)\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\zeta,\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{23}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle\leq\|(u_{\Phi}(0),b_{\Phi}(0))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\frac{2}{25}\eta\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}ds

\displaystyle~~~~~+C(1+\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda)\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\left(\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}(P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}\right)ds.

Taking
\epsilon_{2}
sufficiently small, such that when
\epsilon<\epsilon_{2}<\min\{\epsilon_{*},\epsilon_{1}\}
, we have
1+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}<2,C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}<\frac{1}{10}.

Let
\lambda_{2}
be sufficiently large, and
\lambda
satisfies

\lambda>\lambda_{2}>\max\{10C,\lambda_{1}\}.
So the proposition
3.2
holds.

\square

6.
The Gevrey Estimates of
u_{\Phi}
and
b_{\Phi}

In this section we will give the estimates of
(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})
. In fact with the help of proposition
4.1
and proposition
5.1
, we can directly obtain the estimates of
(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})
with the following results:

Proposition 6.1
.

Let
\kappa\in(0,2)
be a given constant. Let
(u,b)
be a sufficiently smooth solution of the equation (
2.2
) on
[0,T_{*}]
and
(u,b)
decays rapidly to
0
as
y
tends to
+\infty
. Let
a(t)
be a non-negative and non-decreasing function on
\mathbb{R}_{+}
. Then when
\alpha\leq\min\{\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4},\frac{2}{3}\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{2},\frac{1}{2}\gamma_{0}+\frac{5}{8}\}
and
\epsilon<\epsilon_{*}
, for any
t<T_{*}
and sufficiently small
\eta>0
the following inequality holds,

(6.1)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &~~~~~\|\sqrt{a}(u_{\Phi}(t),b_{\Phi}(t))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}-2\int_{0}^{t}\left(\|\sqrt{a^{\prime}}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+\|\sqrt{a^{\prime}}(\zeta,\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\right)ds\\
&~~~~~+2(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{1}{10}\eta)\int_{0}^{t}\left(\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\right)ds\\
&~~~~~+2\left(\lambda-C(1+\lambda\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\right)\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}ds\\
&\leq 2\|\sqrt{a}(0)(u_{\Phi}(0),b_{\Phi}(0))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+C(1+\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda)\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}ds,\end{aligned}

where
l_{\kappa}=\frac{\kappa(2-\kappa)}{4}\in(0,\frac{1}{4}].

Proof.

By (
5.13
), when
\epsilon<\epsilon_{*}
, we have

(6.2)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\sqrt{a}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\leq\|\sqrt{a}(\zeta,\tilde{\zeta})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{23}{3},0}}+\|\sqrt{a}\mathcal{U}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}.\end{split}

Similarly,

(6.3)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\sqrt{a}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}\leq\|\sqrt{a}(\zeta,\tilde{\zeta})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\|\sqrt{a}\mathcal{U}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}.\end{split}

It’s easy to know that when
0\leq s\leq q+t
,
s^{2}\leq 2q^{2}+2t^{2}
holds.
Combining (
4.1
),
(
5.4
), (
6.2
) and (
6.3
), we can complete the proof of proposition
6.1
.
∎

Now we can prove proposition
3.3
by using proposition
6.1
.

Proof of Proposition
3.3
:

Take
a(t)=\langle t\rangle^{2l_{\kappa}-2\eta}
in (
6.1
). By
lemma
2.7
, we can derive

\displaystyle-2(2l_{\kappa}-2\eta)\int_{0}^{t}\left(\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta-\frac{1}{2}}(\zeta,\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\right)ds

\displaystyle+2(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{1}{10}\eta)\int_{0}^{t}\left(\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\right)ds

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle-4(2l_{\kappa}-2\eta)\int_{0}^{t}\left(\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\right)ds

\displaystyle+2(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{1}{10}\eta)\int_{0}^{t}\left(\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\right)ds

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 2\eta\int_{0}^{t}\left(\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\right)ds

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\eta\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}u_{\Phi},\partial_{y}b_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}ds

here we use (
5.16
) and
\epsilon<\epsilon_{2}<\epsilon_{*}.

Thus when
\epsilon<\epsilon_{2}
and
\lambda>\lambda_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\langle t\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}(u_{\Phi}(t),b_{\Phi}(t))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle+\eta\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}u_{\Phi},\partial_{y}b_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\|(u_{\Phi}(0),b_{\Phi}(0))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\frac{1}{5}\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}(P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}ds,

so proposition
3.3
holds.

\square

7.
The Gevrey Estimates of
P_{\Phi}
and
N_{\Phi}

In this section we give a priori estimates for
(P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})
. First, from (
1
),(
2.2
) and (
2.4
) we can obtain that
(P,N)
satisfies the following equation

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\left(\partial_{t}+u\partial_{x}+v\partial_{y}-\partial_{y}^{2}\right)N=-\frac{\ddot{\theta}}{\dot{\theta}}N+b\partial_{x}P+h\partial_{y}P+H\\
\left(\partial_{t}+u\partial_{x}+v\partial_{y}-\kappa\partial_{y}^{2}\right)P=-\frac{\ddot{\theta}}{\dot{\theta}}P+b\partial_{x}N+h\partial_{y}N+\tilde{H}.\end{array}\right.

where
(H,\tilde{H})
is defined as follows

(7.3)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&H=\frac{2}{\dot{\theta}}(\partial_{x}b\partial_{y}^{2}u-\partial_{y}b\partial_{y}\partial_{x}u)+\frac{\kappa-1}{\dot{\theta}}(\partial_{x}u\partial_{y}^{2}b-\partial_{y}u\partial_{y}\partial_{x}b),\\
&\tilde{H}=\frac{2\kappa}{\dot{\theta}}(\partial_{x}b\partial_{y}^{2}b-\partial_{y}b\partial_{y}\partial_{x}b).\end{split}

Next, we use
e^{\Phi(t,D_{x})}
to act on (
7
), and do Bony’s decomposition of all nonlinear terms using (
2.20
). Similar to (
3.1
), we write the equation in the following form:

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\mathcal{L}N_{\Phi}=-\frac{\ddot{\theta}}{\dot{\theta}}N_{\Phi}+T^{\mathrm{h}}_{b}\partial_{x}P_{\Phi}+\frac{2}{3}\delta(t)T^{\mathrm{h}}_{D_{x}b}Q(D_{x})\partial_{x}P_{\Phi}\\
~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~+T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}P}h_{\Phi}+T^{\mathrm{h}}_{h}\partial_{y}P_{\Phi}-T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}N}v_{\Phi}-S+H_{\Phi}\\
\mathcal{L_{\kappa}}P_{\Phi}=-\frac{\ddot{\theta}}{\dot{\theta}}P_{\Phi}+T^{\mathrm{h}}_{b}\partial_{x}N_{\Phi}+\frac{2}{3}\delta(t)T^{\mathrm{h}}_{D_{x}b}Q(D_{x})\partial_{x}N_{\Phi}\\
~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~+T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}N}h_{\Phi}+T^{\mathrm{h}}_{h}\partial_{y}N_{\Phi}-T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}P}v_{\Phi}-\tilde{S}+\tilde{H}_{\Phi}\\
(P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})|_{y=0}=(0,0),~(P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})|_{y\rightarrow+\infty}=(0,0),\\
(P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})|_{t=0}=\left(\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}e^{\delta[D_{x}]^{\frac{2}{3}}}(b_{0}\partial_{x}b_{0}+h_{0}\partial_{y}b_{0}),\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}e^{\delta[D_{x}]^{\frac{2}{3}}}(b_{0}\partial_{x}u_{0}+h_{0}\partial_{y}u_{0})\right).\end{array}\right.

where
(S,\tilde{S})
is defined as

(7.10)

\displaystyle\begin{split}S=&\left(T_{u}^{\mathrm{h}}\partial_{x}N\right)_{\Phi}-T_{u}^{\mathrm{h}}\partial_{x}N_{\Phi}-\frac{2}{3}\delta(t)T_{D_{x}u}^{\mathrm{h}}Q(D_{x})\partial_{x}N_{\Phi}\\
&+\left(T_{\partial_{y}N}^{\mathrm{h}}v\right)_{\Phi}-T_{\partial_{y}N}^{\mathrm{h}}v_{\Phi}+\left(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{v}\partial_{y}N\right)_{\Phi}-T^{\mathrm{h}}_{v}\partial_{y}N_{\Phi}\\
&+\left(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{x}N}u+R^{\mathrm{h}}(u,\partial_{x}N)+R^{\mathrm{h}}(v,\partial_{y}N)\right)_{\Phi}\\
&-\left(T_{\partial_{x}P}b+R^{\mathrm{h}}(b,\partial_{x}P)+R^{\mathrm{h}}(h,\partial_{y}P)\right)_{\Phi}\\
&-(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{b}\partial_{x}P)_{\Phi}+T^{\mathrm{h}}_{b}\partial_{x}P_{\Phi}+\frac{2}{3}\delta(t)T^{\mathrm{h}}_{D_{x}b}Q(D_{x})\partial_{x}P_{\Phi}\\
&-(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}P}h)_{\Phi}+T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}P}h_{\Phi}-(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{h}\partial_{y}P)_{\Phi}+T^{\mathrm{h}}_{h}\partial_{y}P_{\Phi},\end{split}

(7.11)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\tilde{S}=&\left(T_{u}^{\mathrm{h}}\partial_{x}P\right)_{\Phi}-T_{u}^{\mathrm{h}}\partial_{x}P_{\Phi}-\frac{2}{3}\delta(t)T_{D_{x}u}^{\mathrm{h}}Q(D_{x})\partial_{x}P_{\Phi}\\
&+\left(T_{\partial_{y}P}^{\mathrm{h}}v\right)_{\Phi}-T_{\partial_{y}P}^{\mathrm{h}}v_{\Phi}+\left(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{v}\partial_{y}P\right)_{\Phi}-T^{\mathrm{h}}_{v}\partial_{y}P_{\Phi}\\
&+\left(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{x}P}u+R^{\mathrm{h}}(u,\partial_{x}P)+R^{\mathrm{h}}(v,\partial_{y}P)\right)_{\Phi}\\
&-\left(T_{\partial_{x}N}b+R^{\mathrm{h}}(b,\partial_{x}N)+R^{\mathrm{h}}(h,\partial_{y}N)\right)_{\Phi}\\
&-(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{b}\partial_{x}N)_{\Phi}+T^{\mathrm{h}}_{b}\partial_{x}N_{\Phi}+\frac{2}{3}\delta(t)T^{\mathrm{h}}_{D_{x}b}Q(D_{x})\partial_{x}N_{\Phi}\\
&-(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}N}h)_{\Phi}+T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}N}h_{\Phi}-(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{h}\partial_{y}N)_{\Phi}+T^{\mathrm{h}}_{h}\partial_{y}N_{\Phi}.\end{split}

First, we give the estimates of
(S,\tilde{S})
.

Lemma 7.1
.

Let
(S,\tilde{S})
be given by (
7.10
) and (
7.11
), respectively, and
\kappa\in(0,2)
. Then for any
s>0,
we have:

\displaystyle\|(S,\tilde{S})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|(P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{1}{3},0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-2\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{2}}\|(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|(\partial_{y}P_{\Phi},\partial_{y}N_{\Phi})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}.

Proof.

First, by lemma
2.6
and lemma
4.1
, we get

\displaystyle\left\|\left(T_{u}^{\mathrm{h}}\partial_{x}N\right)_{\Phi}-T_{u}^{\mathrm{h}}\partial_{x}N_{\Phi}-\frac{2}{3}\delta(t)T_{D_{x}u}^{\mathrm{h}}Q(D_{x})\partial_{x}N_{\Phi}\right\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|u_{\Phi}\|_{L_{\mathrm{v}}^{\infty}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{5}{2}+})}\|N_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{1}{3},0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|N_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{1}{3},0}}.

Using
2.5
, it yields

\displaystyle\left\|\left(T_{\partial_{y}N}^{\mathrm{h}}v\right)_{\Phi}-T_{\partial_{y}N}^{\mathrm{h}}v_{\Phi}\right\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\partial_{y}N_{\Phi}\|_{L_{\mathrm{v}}^{2}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{3}{2}+})}\|v_{\Phi}\|_{L_{\mathrm{v},\Psi}^{\infty}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{s-\frac{1}{3}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\langle t\rangle^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}N_{\Phi}\|_{L_{\mathrm{v}}^{2}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{3}{2}+})}\|u_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}.

And applying (
2.4
) and (
3.14
), we have

\displaystyle\partial_{y}N_{\Phi}

\displaystyle=\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha}(\partial_{y}b\partial_{x}u+b\partial_{y}\partial_{x}u+\partial_{y}h\partial_{y}u+h\partial_{y}^{2}u)_{\Phi}

\displaystyle=\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha}(\partial_{y}b\partial_{x}u+b\partial_{y}\partial_{x}u-\partial_{x}b\partial_{y}u+h\partial_{y}^{2}u)_{\Phi}

Thus by (
2.20
) and lemma
4.1
, we get

(7.12)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\partial_{y}N_{\Phi}\|_{L_{\mathrm{v}}^{2}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{3}{2}+})}&=\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha}\|(\partial_{y}b\partial_{x}u+b\partial_{y}\partial_{x}u+\partial_{y}h\partial_{y}u+h\partial_{y}^{2}u)_{\Phi}\|_{L_{\mathrm{v}}^{2}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{3}{2}+})}\\
&\leq\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha}C\epsilon^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-2\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\\
&=C\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-2\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}.\end{aligned}

Then we can deduce that

\displaystyle\left\|\left(T_{\partial_{y}N}^{\mathrm{h}}v\right)_{\Phi}-T_{\partial_{y}N}^{\mathrm{h}}v_{\Phi}\right\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-2\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{2}}\|u_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}.

Next, using lemma
2.5
and lemma
4.1
, it gives

\displaystyle\left\|\left(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{v}\partial_{y}N\right)_{\Phi}-T^{\mathrm{h}}_{v}\partial_{y}N_{\Phi}\right\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|v_{\Phi}\|_{L_{\mathrm{v}}^{\infty}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{3}{2}+})}\|\partial_{y}N_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s-\frac{1}{3},0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}N_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s-\frac{1}{3},0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}N_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}.

Similarly, by lemma
2.2
and lemma
4.1
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\left\|R^{\mathrm{h}}(v,\partial_{y}N)_{\Psi}\right\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|v_{\Phi}\|_{L_{\mathrm{v}}^{\infty}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|\partial_{y}N_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}N_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}.

We can derive from lemma
2.2
and lemma
4.1

\displaystyle\left\|\left(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{x}N}u+R^{\mathrm{h}}(u,\partial_{x}N)\right)_{\Phi}\right\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}\leq C\|N_{\Phi}\|_{L_{\mathrm{v}}^{\infty}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{3}{2}+})}\|u_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}},

Combining (
2.4
), (
2.20
), (
3.14
) and lemma
4.1
, we find

(7.13)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|N_{\Phi}\|_{L_{\mathrm{v}}^{\infty}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{3}{2}+})}&=\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha}\|(b\partial_{x}u+h\partial_{y}u)_{\Phi}\|_{L_{\mathrm{v}}^{\infty}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{3}{2}+})}\\
&\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-2\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{2}},\end{aligned}

Thus we can get

\displaystyle\left\|\left(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{x}N}u+R^{\mathrm{h}}(u,\partial_{x}N)\right)_{\Phi}\right\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-2\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{2}}\|u_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}.

Looking closely at (
7.10
), we can find that the remaining terms are similar, so we can get the estimates of
S
.

\displaystyle\|S\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|(P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{1}{3},0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-2\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{2}}\|(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|(\partial_{y}P_{\Phi},\partial_{y}N_{\Phi})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}.

On the other hand the estimates of
\tilde{S}
are similar, so we complete the proof of lemma
7.1
.
∎

Now we give the a priori estimates of
(P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})
, and the exact results are shown below.

Proposition 7.1
.

Let
\kappa\in(0,2)
be a given constant. Let
(P,N)
be defined by (
2.4
) and
a(t)
be a non-negative and non-decreasing function on
\mathbb{R}_{+}
. Then when
\alpha\leq\min\{\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4},\frac{2}{3}\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{2},\frac{1}{2}\gamma_{0}+\frac{5}{8}\}
and
\epsilon<\epsilon_{*}
, for any
t<T_{*}
and sufficiently small
\eta>0
the following equation holds:

(7.14)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &~~~~~\|\sqrt{a}(P_{\Phi}(t),N_{\Phi}(t))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}-\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a^{\prime}}(P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}ds\\
&~~~~~+(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{1}{10}\eta-C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}P_{\Phi},\partial_{y}N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}ds\\
&~~~~~+2\left(\lambda-C(1+\lambda\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\right)\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}ds\\
&\leq\|\sqrt{a}(0)(P_{\Phi}(0),N_{\Phi}(0))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}+C\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\left(\|\sqrt{a}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\|\sqrt{a}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\right)ds\\
&~~~~~+(\frac{3}{25}\eta+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\int_{0}^{t}\left(\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\right)ds,\end{aligned}

where
l_{\kappa}=\frac{\kappa(2-\kappa)}{4}\in(0,\frac{1}{4}].

Proof.

Take
H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}
inner product of
\eqref{PNp}_{1}
and
\eqref{PNp}_{2}
with
a(t)N_{\Phi}
and
a(t)P_{\Phi}
respectively,
and add them together.

(7.15)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &a(\mathcal{L}N_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}+a(\mathcal{L_{\kappa}}P_{\Phi},P_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}\\
=&-a(\frac{\ddot{\theta}}{\dot{\theta}}N_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}-a(\frac{\ddot{\theta}}{\dot{\theta}}P_{\Phi},P_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}+a(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{b}\partial_{x}P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}+a(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{b}\partial_{x}N_{\Phi},P_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}\\
&+a(\frac{2}{3}\delta(t)T^{\mathrm{h}}_{D_{x}b}Q(D_{x})\partial_{x}P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}+a(\frac{2}{3}\delta(t)T^{\mathrm{h}}_{D_{x}b}Q(D_{x})\partial_{x}N_{\Phi},P_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}\\
&+a(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}P}h_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}+a(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}N}h_{\Phi},P_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}\\
&+a(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{h}\partial_{y}P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}+a(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{h}\partial_{y}N_{\Phi},P_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}\\
&-a(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}N}v_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}-a(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}P}v_{\Phi},P_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}\\
&-a(S,N_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}-a(\tilde{S},P_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}+a(H_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}+a(\tilde{H}_{\Phi},P_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}\\
=&\sum_{i=1}^{16}M_{i}.\end{aligned}

For
M_{1}
, by (
2.4
), (
2.20
) and (
3.14
), we have

\displaystyle M_{1}

\displaystyle=\alpha\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-1}a\left((b\partial_{x}u+h\partial_{y}u)_{\Phi},N_{\Phi}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}

\displaystyle=\alpha\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-1}a\left((T^{\mathrm{h}}_{b}\partial_{x}u)_{\Phi}+(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{x}u}b)_{\Phi}+R^{\mathrm{h}}(b,\partial_{x}u)_{\Phi}\right.

\displaystyle~~~~~\left.+(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{h}\partial_{y}u)_{\Phi}+(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}h)_{\Phi}+R^{\mathrm{h}}(h,\partial_{y}u)_{\Phi},N_{\Phi}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{6}K_{i}.

For
K_{1}
,
K_{2}
and
K_{3}
, it’s obvious

\displaystyle|K_{1}+K_{2}+K_{3}|

\displaystyle\leq C\alpha\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-1}\|(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{3}{2}+})}\|\sqrt{a}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\langle t\rangle^{2\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{4}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+C\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}.

Similarly, for
K_{5}
and
K_{6}
, we have

\displaystyle|K_{5}+K_{6}|

\displaystyle\leq C\alpha\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-1}\|\partial_{y}u_{\Phi}\|_{L^{2}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|\sqrt{a}h_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{19}{3}})}\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\langle t\rangle^{2\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{4}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}b_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+C\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}.

For
K_{4}
, when
\epsilon<\epsilon_{*}
,
C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}<1
holds. Thus applying (
5.16
), we get

(7.16)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}u_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}\leq\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}.\end{split}

Then we deduce

\displaystyle|K_{4}|

\displaystyle\leq C\alpha\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-1}\|h_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}u_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\left(\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}\right)\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{3\alpha-2\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}.

Combining
K_{1}-K_{6}
, we can obtain

\displaystyle|M_{1}|\leq

\displaystyle C\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+C(1+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}.

For
M_{2}
, it’s similar that

\displaystyle|M_{2}|\leq

\displaystyle C\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+C(1+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}P_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}.

For
M_{3}
and
M_{4}
, using lemma
2.4
, we get

\displaystyle|M_{3}+M_{4}|

\displaystyle\leq C\|b\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{3}{2}+})}\|\sqrt{a}P_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}P_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}},

here we use the fact that
\alpha\leq\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}.
For
M_{5}
and
M_{6}
, we can deduce along the same line

\displaystyle|M_{5}+M_{6}|\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}.

For
M_{7}
, first it’s easy to prove that (
7.12
) also holds for
\partial_{y}P
, then we have

\displaystyle|M_{7}|

\displaystyle\leq C\|\partial_{y}P\|_{L^{2}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|\sqrt{a}h_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v},\Psi}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{19}{3}})}\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-2\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\langle t\rangle^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{2\alpha-2\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}b_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+C\epsilon\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}.

For
M_{8}
,
M_{11}
and
M_{12}
, from a similar derivation we can find

\displaystyle|M_{8}+M_{11}+M_{12}|\leq C\epsilon\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+C\epsilon\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}.

For
M_{9}
, we have

\displaystyle|M_{9}|

\displaystyle\leq C\|h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}P_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}P_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-2\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{2}}\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}P_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-2\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}P_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}P_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}},

here we use
\alpha\leq\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}
and
\gamma_{0}>1.

For
M_{10}
, it’s similar that

\displaystyle|M_{10}|\leq C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}P_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}N_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}.

For
M_{13},
by lemma
7.1
, we can know

\displaystyle|M_{13}|

\displaystyle\leq\|\sqrt{a}S\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{19}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}(P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}+\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-2\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{2}}\|\sqrt{a}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}\right.

\displaystyle~~~~~\left.+\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}P_{\Phi},\partial_{y}N_{\Phi})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{19}{3},0}}\right)\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{2\alpha-2\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle~~~~~+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}P_{\Phi},\partial_{y}N_{\Phi})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+C\epsilon\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+C\epsilon\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle~~~~~+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}P_{\Phi},\partial_{y}N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-2\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}})\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+C\epsilon\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}

\displaystyle~~~~~+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}P_{\Phi},\partial_{y}N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}.

For
M_{14}
, along the same line we have

\displaystyle|M_{14}|

\displaystyle\leq C(\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}})\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+C\epsilon\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}

\displaystyle~~~~~+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}P_{\Phi},\partial_{y}N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}.

For
M_{15}
and
M_{16}
, they can be estimated by similar derivations. We only give the estimate procedure for
M_{16}
here. Notice that

\displaystyle\tilde{H}=\frac{2\kappa}{\dot{\theta}}(\partial_{x}b\partial_{y}^{2}b-\partial_{y}b\partial_{y}\partial_{x}b)=2\kappa\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha}(\partial_{x}b\partial_{y}^{2}b-\partial_{y}b\partial_{y}\partial_{x}b),

thus

\displaystyle\tilde{H}_{\Phi}=2\kappa\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha}(\partial_{x}b\partial_{y}^{2}b)_{\Phi}-2\kappa\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha}(\partial_{y}b\partial_{y}\partial_{x}b)_{\Phi}.

By (
2.20
), we have

\displaystyle(\partial_{y}b\partial_{y}\partial_{x}b)_{\Phi}=\left(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}b}\partial_{y}\partial_{x}b+T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}\partial_{x}b}\partial_{y}b+R^{\mathrm{h}}(\partial_{y}b,\partial_{y}\partial_{x}b)\right)_{\Phi}.

Therefore

\displaystyle\left|\left(2\kappa\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha}a(\partial_{y}b\partial_{y}\partial_{x}b)_{\Phi},P_{\Phi}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|2\kappa\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha}a\left((T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}b}\partial_{y}\partial_{x}b)_{\Phi}+(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}\partial_{x}b}\partial_{y}b)_{\Phi}+R^{\mathrm{h}}(\partial_{y}b,\partial_{y}\partial_{x}b)_{\Phi},P_{\Phi}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\kappa\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha}a\|(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}b}\partial_{y}\partial_{x}b)_{\Phi}+(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}\partial_{x}b}\partial_{y}b)_{\Phi}+R^{\mathrm{h}}(\partial_{y}b,\partial_{y}\partial_{x}b)_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{19}{3},0}}\|P_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\kappa\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha}\|\partial_{y}b_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{3}{2}+})}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}P_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}P_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}.

Next using (
5.14
), similar to (
5.15
), we can get

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\leq\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\sqrt{a}\mathcal{U}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}.

Let
\epsilon<\epsilon_{*}
, then
C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}<1
, thus we can get

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\leq\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\sqrt{a}\mathcal{U}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}.

So

\displaystyle\left|\left(2\kappa\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}a(\partial_{y}b\partial_{y}\partial_{x}b)_{\Phi},P_{\Phi}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}P_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}\mathcal{U}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}P_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{3\alpha-2\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}P_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle+C\langle t\rangle^{2\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{4}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+C\langle t\rangle^{2\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{4}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}P_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+C(1+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}P_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+C\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}},

here we use the fact that
\alpha\leq\min\{\frac{2}{3}\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{2},\frac{1}{2}\gamma_{0}+\frac{5}{8}\}
.
By (
2.20
), we have

\displaystyle 2\kappa\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha}(\partial_{x}b\partial_{y}^{2}b)_{\Phi}=2\kappa\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha}\left((T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{x}b}\partial_{y}^{2}b)_{\Phi}+(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}^{2}b}\partial_{x}b)_{\Phi}+R^{\mathrm{h}}(\partial_{x}b,\partial_{y}^{2}b)_{\Phi}\right).

Applying lemma
2.2
and lemma
4.1
, we can derive

\displaystyle\left|\left(2\kappa\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha}a\left((T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}^{2}b}\partial_{x}b)_{\Phi}+R^{\mathrm{h}}(\partial_{x}b,\partial_{y}^{2}b)_{\Phi}\right),P_{\Phi}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha}\|\partial_{y}^{2}b_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|\sqrt{a}b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}P_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\langle t\rangle^{2\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{4}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}P_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}b_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+C\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}P_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}.

Noticing the boundary condition
P_{\Phi}|_{y=0}=0
, applying integrating by parts, we can gain

\displaystyle\left|\left(2\kappa\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha}a(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{x}b}\partial_{y}^{2}b)_{\Phi},P_{\Phi}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha}a\left|\left((T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}\partial_{x}b}\partial_{y}b)_{\Phi},P_{\Phi}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}\right|+C\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha}a\left|\left((T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{x}b}\partial_{y}b)_{\Phi},\partial_{y}P_{\Phi}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}\right|

\displaystyle+C\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha}a\left|\left((T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{x}b}\partial_{y}b)_{\Phi},\frac{y}{2\langle t\rangle}P_{\Phi}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}.

For
I_{1}
, by (
7.16
), we know

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha}\|\partial_{y}b_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{3}{2}+})}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}\|\sqrt{a}P_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}P_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle~~~~~+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}\|\sqrt{a}P_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{3\alpha-2\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}P_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}P_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}.

For
I_{2}
, from a similar derivation, we obtain

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha}\|b_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{3}{2}+})}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}P_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}P_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}

\displaystyle~~~~~+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}P_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}P_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}.

For
I_{3},
it’s similar,

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\sqrt{a}\frac{y}{2\langle t\rangle}P_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}.

On the other hand, by lemma
2.7
, we have

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}P_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}.

Combining the above estimates, we can obtain

\displaystyle|M_{16}|\leq

\displaystyle C(1+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}P_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+C\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}b_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+(\frac{1}{50}\eta+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+(\frac{1}{100}\eta+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}P_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}.

For
M_{15}
, it’s similar to
M_{16}
.

\displaystyle|M_{15}|\leq

\displaystyle C(1+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}N_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+C\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+(\frac{1}{50}\eta+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+(\frac{1}{100}\eta+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}N_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}.

Next we need to estimate the left side of (
7.15
). Similar to (
4.5
), we have

(7.17)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&a(\mathcal{L}N_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}+a(\mathcal{L_{\kappa}}P_{\Phi},P_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}\\
\geq&\frac{1}{2}\dfrac{d}{dt}\|\sqrt{a}(P_{\Phi}(t),N_{\Phi}(t))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}-\frac{1}{2}\|\sqrt{a^{\prime}}(P_{\Phi}(t),N_{\Phi}(t))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}\\
&+\lambda\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(P_{\Phi}(t),N_{\Phi}(t))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}+2l_{\kappa}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}P_{\Phi}(t),\partial_{y}N_{\Phi}(t))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}\\
&+a\left(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{u}\partial_{x}P_{\Phi},P_{\Phi}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}+a\left(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{u}\partial_{x}N_{\Phi},N_{\Phi}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}\\
&+a\left(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{v}\partial_{y}P_{\Phi},P_{\Phi}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}+a\left(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{v}\partial_{y}N_{\Phi},N_{\Phi}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}\\
&+a\left(\frac{2}{3}\delta(t)T^{\mathrm{h}}_{D_{x}u}Q(D_{x})\partial_{x}P_{\Phi},P_{\Phi}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}+a\left(\frac{2}{3}\delta(t)T^{\mathrm{h}}_{D_{x}u}Q(D_{x})\partial_{x}N_{\Phi},N_{\Phi}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}\\
=&\sum_{i=1}^{10}J_{i}.\end{split}

By a similar derivation of
a\left(\mathcal{L}\mathcal{U},\mathcal{U}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}
, we can get

\displaystyle|\sum_{i=5}^{10}J_{i}|\leq C(\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}})\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}P_{\Phi},\partial_{y}N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}.

Combining all the above estimates and integrating over
[0,t]
, we can obtain the following estimates.

\displaystyle~~~~~\|\sqrt{a}(P_{\Phi}(t),N_{\Phi}(t))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}-\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a^{\prime}}(P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle~~~~~+(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{1}{10}\eta-C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}P_{\Phi},\partial_{y}N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle~~~~~+2\left(\lambda-C(1+\lambda\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\right)\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}ds

\displaystyle\leq\|\sqrt{a}(0)(P_{\Phi}(0),N_{\Phi}(0))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}+C\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\left(\|\sqrt{a}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\|\sqrt{a}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\right)ds

\displaystyle~~~~~+(\frac{3}{25}\eta+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\int_{0}^{t}\left(\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\right)ds.

We have thus completed the proof of proposition
7.1
.
∎

Now we can prove proposition
3.4
by applying proposition
7.1
.

Proof of Proposition
3.4
:

By taking
a(t)=\langle t\rangle^{2l_{\kappa}-2\eta}
in (
7.14
), and using lemma
2.7
, we deduce

\displaystyle-(2l_{\kappa}-2\eta)\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta-\frac{1}{2}}(P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle+(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{1}{10}\eta-C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}P_{\Phi},\partial_{y}N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle-2(2l_{\kappa}-2\eta)\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}P_{\Phi},\partial_{y}N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle+(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{1}{10}\eta-C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}P_{\Phi},\partial_{y}N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle(\frac{39}{10}\eta-C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}P_{\Phi},\partial_{y}N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}ds,

So we have

\displaystyle\|\langle t\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}(P_{\Phi}(t),N_{\Phi}(t))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}+(\frac{39}{10}\eta-C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}P_{\Phi},\partial_{y}N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle+2\left(\lambda-C(1+\lambda\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\right)\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}(P_{\Phi},N_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|(P_{\Phi}(0),N_{\Phi}(0))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{20}{3},0}}+C\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\left(\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\right)ds

\displaystyle+(\frac{3}{25}\eta+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\int_{0}^{t}\left(\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}\partial_{y}\mathcal{U}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{7,0}}+\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}\zeta,\partial_{y}\tilde{\zeta})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\right)ds,

Let
\epsilon_{3}
be sufficiently small such that when
\epsilon<\epsilon_{3}<\epsilon_{2}
, we have
C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}<\frac{1}{100}\eta.
And
\lambda
satisfies
\lambda>\lambda_{2}.
Then proposition
3.4
holds.

\square

8.
The Gevrey Estimates of
G_{\Phi}
and
\tilde{G}_{\Phi}

Starting from this section, we will give the estimates for the “good function”
(G,\tilde{G})
. For the sake of brevity in this paper, we give the relevant calculations for
G
, while the part of
\tilde{G}
is obtained in the same way. By acting the operator
e^{\Phi(t,D_{x})}
on (
2.2
) and doing the Bony’s decomposition of all the nonlinear terms, we obtain

(8.1)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &~~~~~\partial_{t}G_{\Phi}+\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)[D_{x}]^{\frac{2}{3}}G_{\Phi}-\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}+\langle t\rangle^{-1}G_{\Phi}+T^{\mathrm{h}}_{u}\partial_{x}G_{\Phi}+T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}G}v_{\Phi}\\
&-\frac{1}{2\langle t\rangle}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}(y\psi)}v_{\Phi}+\frac{y}{\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}v_{\Phi}dz-\dot{\theta}P_{\Phi}+\frac{y}{2\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}\dot{\theta}P_{\Phi}dz=Z,\end{aligned}

here
Z
is defined as follows

(8.2)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} -Z\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathrm{def}}}{{=}}&\left(T_{u}^{\mathrm{h}}\partial_{x}G\right)_{\Phi}-T_{u}^{\mathrm{h}}\partial_{x}G_{\Phi}+\left(T_{\partial_{x}G}^{\mathrm{h}}u\right)_{\Phi}+\left(R^{\mathrm{h}}\left(u,\partial_{x}G\right)\right)_{\Phi}\\
&+\left(T_{\partial_{y}G}^{\mathrm{h}}v\right)_{\Phi}-T_{\partial_{y}G}^{\mathrm{h}}v_{\Phi}+\left(T_{v}^{\mathrm{h}}\partial_{y}G\right)_{\Phi}+\left(R^{\mathrm{h}}\left(v,\partial_{y}G\right)\right)_{\Phi},\\
&-\frac{1}{2\langle t\rangle}\left(\left(T_{\partial_{y}(y\psi)}^{\mathrm{h}}v\right)_{\Phi}-T_{\partial_{y}(y\psi)}^{\mathrm{h}}v_{\Phi}\right)-\frac{1}{2\langle t\rangle}\left(T_{v}^{\mathrm{h}}\partial_{y}(y\psi)+R^{\mathrm{h}}\left(\partial_{y}(y\psi),v\right)\right)_{\Phi}\\
&+\frac{y}{\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}\left(\left(T_{\partial_{y}u}^{\mathrm{h}}v\right)_{\Phi}-T_{\partial_{y}u}^{\mathrm{h}}v_{\Phi}\right)dz+\frac{y}{\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}\left(T_{v}^{\mathrm{h}}\partial_{y}u+R^{\mathrm{h}}\left(v,\partial_{y}u\right)\right)_{\Phi}dz.\end{aligned}

We first give the estimates of source term
Z
.

Lemma 8.1
.

The source term
Z
satisfies the following.

\displaystyle\|Z\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}\leq C\epsilon\left(\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}+\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}\right).

The proof of this lemma can be found in lemma 6.1 of
[
26
]
, just notice the difference of lemma
2.5
in this paper. For the sake of brevity, we will not give the proof here.

With this lemma, we can give the a priori estimates of
G_{\Phi}
, as follows:

Proposition 8.1
.

Let
\kappa\in(0,2)
be a given constant. Let
(G,\tilde{G})
be defined by (
2.5
), and
a(t)
be a non-negative and non-decreasing function on
\mathbb{R}_{+}
. Then when
\alpha\leq\gamma_{0}+\frac{5}{36}
, the following inequality holds for any
t<T_{*}
and sufficiently small
\eta>0
.

(8.3)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\|\sqrt{a}(G_{\Phi}(t),\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(t))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}-\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a^{\prime}}(G_{\Phi},\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}ds+2\int_{0}^{t}\langle s\rangle^{-1}\|\sqrt{a}(G_{\Phi},\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}ds\\
&+2\left(\lambda-C(1+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}})\right)\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}(s)\|\sqrt{a}(G_{\Phi},\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}ds\\
&+(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{2}{25}\eta)\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}ds\\
\leq&\|\sqrt{a}(0)(G_{\Phi}(0),\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(0))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}ds.\end{aligned}

where
l_{\kappa}=\frac{\kappa(2-\kappa)}{4}\in(0,\frac{1}{4}].

Proof.

By taking
H_{\Psi}^{4,0}
inner product of (
8.1
) with

a(t)G_{\Phi}
, and using (
4.4
) we can get

(8.4)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \frac{1}{2}&\frac{d}{dt}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}-\frac{1}{2}\|\sqrt{a^{\prime}}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}+\langle t\rangle^{-1}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}\\
&+\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}+\frac{1}{2}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}\\
\leq&a\left|(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{u}\partial_{x}G_{\Phi},G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}\right|+a\left|(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}G}v_{\Phi},G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}\right|+\frac{a}{2\langle t\rangle}\left|(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}(y\psi)}v_{\Phi},G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}\right|\\
&+a\left|(\frac{y}{\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}v_{\Phi}dz,G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}\right|+a\dot{\theta}\left|(P_{\Phi},G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}\right|\\
&+a\dot{\theta}\left|(\frac{y}{2\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}P_{\Phi}dz,G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}\right|+a\left|(Z,G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}\right|\\
=&\sum_{i=1}^{7}K_{i}.\end{aligned}

For
K_{1}
, by lemma
2.4
, we have

\displaystyle K_{1}\leq

\displaystyle C\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{3}{2}+})}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}.

Next since
\partial_{y}v=-\partial_{x}u
, combining (
4.3
) and lemma
2.8
, we have that for any
s\in\mathbb{R}
, the following holds.

(8.5)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|v_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v},\frac{\Psi}{2}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{s})}\leq C\langle t\rangle^{\frac{1}{4}}\|u_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\frac{\Psi}{2}}^{s+1,0}}\leq C\langle t\rangle^{\frac{1}{4}}\|G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}.\end{aligned}

Then for
K_{2}
, by (
3.15
) we deduce

\displaystyle K_{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\|\partial_{y}G\|_{L^{2}_{\mathrm{v},\frac{\Psi}{2}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|\sqrt{a}v_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v},\frac{\Psi}{2}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{11}{3}})}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}u_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\frac{\Psi}{2}}^{\frac{14}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}.

Using the anisotropic Sobolev inequality and the lemma
2.8
, we can obtain

(8.6)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\sqrt{a}u_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\frac{\Psi}{2}}^{\frac{14}{3},0}}\leq&C\|\sqrt{a}u_{\Phi}\|^{\frac{1}{9}}_{H_{\frac{\Psi}{2}}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}u_{\Phi}\|^{\frac{8}{9}}_{H_{\frac{\Psi}{2}}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}\\
\leq&C\langle t\rangle^{\frac{1}{9}}\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}u_{\Phi}\|^{\frac{1}{9}}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{\frac{8}{9}}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}.\end{aligned}

Thus we can derive

\displaystyle K_{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{36}}\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}u_{\Phi}\|^{\frac{1}{9}}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{\frac{17}{9}}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{36}}\dot{\theta}\left(\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}u_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\left(\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}u_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}\right),

here we use
\alpha\leq\gamma_{0}+\frac{5}{36}.
For
K_{3}
, it follows from lemma
2.8
that

\displaystyle K_{3}\leq

\displaystyle C\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\partial_{y}(y\psi)\|_{L^{2}_{\mathrm{v},\frac{\Psi}{2}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|\sqrt{a}v_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v},\frac{\Psi}{2}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{11}{3}})}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\partial_{y}G\|_{L^{2}_{\mathrm{v},\frac{\Psi}{2}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|\sqrt{a}v_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v},\frac{\Psi}{2}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{11}{3}})}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}},

By the estimates of
K_{2}
, we get

\displaystyle K_{3}\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\left(\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}u_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}\right).

For
K_{4},
first for any
s\in\mathbb{R}
, we have

(8.7)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \left\|\int_{y}^{\infty}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}v_{\Phi}dz\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{s})}&\leq\left\|\int_{y}^{\infty}\|\partial_{y}u\|_{H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+}}\|v_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\mathrm{h}}^{s}}dz\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}}\\
&\leq C\|\partial_{y}u\|_{L^{2}_{\mathrm{v},\frac{3}{4}\Psi}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|e^{\frac{3}{4}\Psi}v_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{s})}\left(\int_{y}^{\infty}e^{-3\Psi}dz\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\leq C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}}e^{-\frac{3}{2}\Psi}\|u_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\frac{3}{4}\Psi}^{1+s,0}},\end{aligned}

Applying (
8.6
), we deduce

\displaystyle K_{4}

\displaystyle\leq a\|\frac{y}{\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}v_{\Phi}dz\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{11}{3},0}}\|G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\frac{y}{\langle t\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}}e^{-\frac{1}{2}\Psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\mathrm{v}}}\|\sqrt{a}u_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\frac{3}{4}\Psi}^{\frac{14}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{7}{18}}\dot{\theta}\left(\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}u_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\left(\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}u_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}\right),

here we use
\alpha\leq\gamma_{0}+\frac{5}{36}.

For
K_{5}
, it follows from (
2.4
), (
2.20
), (
4.3
), lemma
2.2
and lemma
4.1
that for any
s\in\mathbb{R}
,

(8.8)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\dot{\theta}P_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}=&\|(b\partial_{x}b+h\partial_{y}b)_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}\\
\leq&\|b_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v},\frac{\Psi}{2}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{3}{2}+})}\|b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\frac{\Psi}{2}}^{s+1,0}}+\|h_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v},\frac{\Psi}{2}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|\partial_{y}b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\frac{\Psi}{2}}^{s,0}}\\
&+\|\partial_{y}b_{\Phi}\|_{L^{2}_{\mathrm{v},\frac{\Psi}{2}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|h_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v},\frac{\Psi}{2}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{s})}\\
\leq&C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\frac{\Psi}{2}}^{s+1,0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\frac{\Psi}{2}}^{s,0}}.\end{aligned}

Thus for
K_{5}
, we have

\displaystyle K_{5}\leq

\displaystyle Ca\|\dot{\theta}P_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{11}{3},0}}\|G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}a\|b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\frac{\Psi}{2}}^{\frac{14}{3},0}}\|G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}a\|\partial_{y}b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\frac{\Psi}{2}}^{\frac{11}{3},0}}\|G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle K_{51}+K_{52}.

Using the estimates of
K_{2}
, we deduce

\displaystyle K_{51}\leq C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\left(\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}b_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}+\|\sqrt{a}(G_{\Phi},\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}\right).

On the other hand, by using lemma
2.8
, it yields

\displaystyle K_{52}\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{11}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-2\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}.

here we use
\alpha\leq\gamma_{0}+\frac{5}{36}.
Combining
K_{51}
and
K_{52}
, we get

\displaystyle K_{5}\leq

\displaystyle C(\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}})\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(G_{\Phi},\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}b_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}.

For
K_{6}
, first by applying lemma
2.7
, we know that for any
s\in\mathbb{R}
,

(8.9)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\frac{y}{2\langle t\rangle}\int^{\infty}_{y}fdz\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}\leq C\|f\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}},\end{aligned}

Thus similar to
K_{5}
, we have

\displaystyle K_{6}\leq

\displaystyle C(\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}})\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(G_{\Phi},\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}b_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}.

For
K_{7}
, by lemma
8.1
we derive that

\displaystyle K_{7}\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{11}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-2\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{2}})\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{11}{3},0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}})\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{11}{3},0}}.

By taking
K_{1}-K_{7}
into (
8.4
) and integrating over
[0,t]
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}(t)\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}-\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a^{\prime}}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}ds+\int_{0}^{t}\langle t\rangle^{-1}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}ds

\displaystyle+2\left(\lambda-C(1+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}})\right)\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}(s)\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle+(1-\frac{1}{25}\eta)\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{a}(0)G_{\Phi}(0)\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}+C(1+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}})\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}(s)\|\sqrt{a}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}ds+\frac{1}{25}\eta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}ds.

The estimates for
\tilde{G}_{\Phi}
follow in the same way, and we have thus completed the proof of proposition
8.1
.
∎

Now we can prove proposition
3.5
by applying proposition
8.1
.

Proof of Proposition
3.5
:

Take
a(t)=\langle t\rangle^{2+2l_{\kappa}-2\eta}
in (
8.3
). By lemma
2.7
we deduce

\displaystyle-(2+2l_{\kappa}-2\eta)\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{1+l_{\kappa}-\eta-\frac{1}{2}}(G_{\Phi},\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}ds+2\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{1+l_{\kappa}-\eta-\frac{1}{2}}(G_{\Phi},\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}ds

\displaystyle+(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{2}{25}\eta)\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{1+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}ds

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{98}{25}\eta\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{1+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}ds,

then we get

\displaystyle\|\langle t\rangle^{1+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(G_{\Phi}(t),\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(t))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}+\frac{98}{25}\eta\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{1+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}ds

\displaystyle+2\left(\lambda-C(1+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}})\right)\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}(s)\|\langle s\rangle^{1+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(G_{\Phi},\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|(G_{\Phi}(0),\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(0))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}\sqrt{a}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}ds.

Thus when
\epsilon<\epsilon_{3}
and
\lambda>\lambda_{2}
, proposition
3.5
holds.

\square

9.
The Gevrey Estimates of
\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}
and
\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}

In this section, we will give the estimates of
(\partial_{y}G,\partial_{y}\tilde{G})
, for which we act on (
8.1
) with
\partial_{y}
,

(9.1)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &~~~~~\partial_{t}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}+\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)[D_{x}]^{\frac{2}{3}}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}-\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}+\langle t\rangle^{-1}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}+\partial_{y}\left(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{u}\partial_{x}G_{\Phi}+T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}G}v_{\Phi}\right.\\
&\left.-\frac{1}{2\langle t\rangle}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}(y\psi)}v_{\Phi}+\frac{y}{\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}v_{\Phi}dz-\dot{\theta}P_{\Phi}+\frac{y}{2\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}\dot{\theta}P_{\Phi}dz\right)=\partial_{y}Z,\end{aligned}

where
Z
is defined by (
8.2
).

Proposition 9.1
.

Let
\kappa\in(0,2)
be a given constant. Let
(G,\tilde{G})
be defined by (
2.5
), and
a(t)
be a non-negative and non-decreasing function on
\mathbb{R}_{+}
. Then when
\alpha\leq\gamma_{0}+\frac{5}{36}
, the following inequality holds for any
t<T_{*}
and sufficiently small
\eta>0
.

(9.2)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}(t),\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(t))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}-\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a^{\prime}}(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}ds\\
&+2\int_{0}^{t}\langle s\rangle^{-1}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}ds+(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{7}{25}\eta)\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}ds\\
&+2(\lambda-C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}})\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}ds\\
\leq&\|\sqrt{a}(0)(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}(0),\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(0))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{a}(G_{\Phi},\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}ds,\end{aligned}

where
l_{\kappa}=\frac{\kappa(2-\kappa)}{4}\in(0,\frac{1}{4}].

Proof.

First noticing
u|_{y=0}=v|_{y=0}=P|_{y=0}=G|_{y=0}
, combining
(
2.2
) and (
8.2
), we have

(9.3)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}|_{y=0}=\partial_{y}^{2}G|_{y=0}=0,~~P_{\Phi}|_{y=0}=0,~~Z|_{y=0}=0\\
&\left(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{u}\partial_{x}G_{\Phi}+T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}G}v_{\Phi}-\frac{1}{2\langle t\rangle}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}(y\psi)}v_{\Phi}+\frac{y}{\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}v_{\Phi}dz+\frac{y}{2\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}\dot{\theta}P_{\Phi}dz\right)|_{y=0}=0\end{aligned}

These boundary conditions ensure that no boundary term arises in integrating by parts.

Taking
H_{\Psi}^{3,0}
inner product of (
9.1
) with
a(t)\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}
and integrating by parts, we derive

(9.4)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \frac{1}{2}&\frac{d}{dt}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}-\frac{1}{2}\|\sqrt{a^{\prime}}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+\langle t\rangle^{-1}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\\
&+\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}+\frac{1}{2}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\\
\leq&a\left|(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{u}\partial_{x}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|+a\left|(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}G}v_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|\\
&+\frac{a}{2\langle t\rangle}\left|(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}(y\psi)}v_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|+a\left|(\frac{y}{\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}v_{\Phi}dz,\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|\\
&+a\dot{\theta}\left|(P_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|+a\dot{\theta}\left|(\frac{y}{2\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}P_{\Phi}dz,\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|\\
