&+a\left|(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{u}\partial_{x}G_{\Phi},\frac{y}{2\langle t\rangle}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|+a\left|(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}G}v_{\Phi},\frac{y}{2\langle t\rangle}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|\\
&+\frac{a}{2\langle t\rangle}\left|(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}(y\psi)}v_{\Phi},\frac{y}{2\langle t\rangle}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|+a\left|(\frac{y}{\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}v_{\Phi}dz,\frac{y}{2\langle t\rangle}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|\\
&+a\dot{\theta}\left|(P_{\Phi},\frac{y}{2\langle t\rangle}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|+a\dot{\theta}\left|(\frac{y}{2\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}P_{\Phi}dz,\frac{y}{2\langle t\rangle}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|\\
&+a\left|(Z,\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|+a\left|(Z,\frac{y}{2\langle t\rangle}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|\\
=&\sum_{i=1}^{14}L_{i}.\end{aligned}

For
L_{1},
it follows from lemma
2.2
and lemma
4.1
that

\displaystyle L_{1}\leq

\displaystyle C\|\sqrt{a}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{u}\partial_{x}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-2\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}},

here we use
\alpha\leq\gamma_{0}+\frac{5}{36}
and
\gamma_{0}>1.
For
L_{2}
, applying lemma
2.2
, (
3.15
) and (
8.5
), we deduce

\displaystyle L_{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\|\partial_{y}G\|_{L^{2}_{\mathrm{v},\frac{1}{2}\Psi}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|\sqrt{a}v_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v},\frac{1}{2}\Psi}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{3})}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}.

Then similar to
L_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle L_{2}\leq\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}.

For
L_{3}
, noticing lemma
2.8
, we can use a similar derivation of
L_{2}
to get

\displaystyle L_{3}\leq\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}.

For
L_{4},
by (
8.7
), we can gain

\displaystyle L_{4}\leq

\displaystyle Ca\|\frac{y}{\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}v_{\Phi}dz\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{2}}\|\frac{y}{\langle t\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}}e^{-\frac{1}{2}\Psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\mathrm{v}}}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}.

Therefore similarly we have

\displaystyle L_{4}\leq\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}.

For
L_{5},
by lemma
2.8
and (
8.8
) we obtain

\displaystyle L_{5}\leq

\displaystyle\left(C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\frac{1}{2}\Psi}^{4,0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\frac{1}{2}\Psi}^{3,0}}\right)\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right)\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-2\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{a}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-2\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{a}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}.

For
L_{6}
, by using (
8.9
) and the estimates of
L_{5}
, we have

\displaystyle L_{6}\leq\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{a}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}.

For
L_{7}-L_{12}
, using (
8.9
) and the estimates of
L_{1}-L_{6}
, we can directly get

\displaystyle\sum_{i=7}^{12}L_{i}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{3}{50}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{a}(G_{\Phi},\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}.

For
L_{13},
by applying lemma
8.1
, we can derive

\displaystyle L_{13}\leq

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{a}Z\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\left(\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{11}{3},0}}+\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right)\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-2\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\left(\|\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}+\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\left(\|\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}+\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}\right).

here we use
\alpha\leq\gamma_{0}+\frac{5}{36}
and
\gamma_{0}>1.

For
L_{14}
, similarly by applying (
8.9
), we can deduce

\displaystyle L_{14}\leq\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\left(\|\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}+\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}\right).

Taking
L_{1}-L_{14}
into (
9.4
), and integrating over
[0,t]
, we can get the estimates of
\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}
.

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}(t)\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}-\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a^{\prime}}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}ds+2\int_{0}^{t}\langle s\rangle^{-1}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}ds

\displaystyle+2\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}ds+\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{a}(0)\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}(0)\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle+\frac{7}{25}\eta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}ds+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{a}(G_{\Phi},\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}ds.

This completes the estimates of
\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}
. And the estimates of
\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}
can be obtained in a similar way. Thus we have completed the proof of proposition
9.1
.
∎

Now we can prove proposition
3.6
.

Proof of Proposition
3.6
:

Taking
a(t)=\langle t\rangle^{3+2l_{\kappa}-2\eta}
in (
9.2
), we have

\displaystyle\|\langle t\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}(t),\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(t))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{7}{25}\eta)\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}ds

\displaystyle+2(\lambda-C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}})\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}(0),\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(0))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{1+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(G_{\Phi},\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle+(1+2\l_{\kappa}-2\eta)\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{1+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}ds.

And using (
3.20
) we can deduce

\displaystyle\|\langle t\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}(t),\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(t))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{7}{25}\eta)\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}ds

\displaystyle+2(\lambda-C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}})\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\eta^{-1}\left(\|(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}(0),\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(0))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+\|(G_{\Phi}(0),\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(0))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}\right)

\displaystyle+C\eta^{-1}\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}\sqrt{a}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}ds,

thus when
\epsilon<\epsilon_{3}
and
\lambda>\lambda_{2}
, proposition
3.6
holds.

\square

10.
The Gevrey Estimates of
\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}
and
\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}

Next, we estimate
(\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})
. Unlike
[
26
]
, we need to do Gevrey norm estimates, whereas in
[
26
]
they only need Sobolev norm estimates. This is because the
(P,N)
term due to the magnetic field leads us to estimate Gevrey norm of
(\partial_{y}^{2}u_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}b_{\Phi})
, which can be found in the estimates of
M_{16}
in section
7
.

Acting
\partial_{y}
on (
9.1
), we can get

(10.1)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &~~~~~\partial_{t}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}+\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)[D_{x}]^{\frac{2}{3}}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}-\partial_{y}^{4}G_{\Phi}+\langle t\rangle^{-1}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}+\partial_{y}^{2}\left(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{u}\partial_{x}G_{\Phi}+T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}G}v_{\Phi}\right.\\
&\left.-\frac{1}{2\langle t\rangle}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}(y\psi)}v_{\Phi}+\frac{y}{\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}v_{\Phi}dz-\dot{\theta}P_{\Phi}+\frac{y}{2\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}\dot{\theta}P_{\Phi}dz\right)=\partial_{y}^{2}Z,\end{aligned}

and the boundary condition is

(10.2)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}|_{y=0}=0.\end{aligned}

First we give the estimates of
\partial_{y}^{2}Z
.

Lemma 10.1
.

The source term
\partial_{y}^{2}Z
satisfise

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}^{2}Z\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{4}}\|G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}.

Proof.

We estimate them term by term. First use lemma
2.5
and lemma
4.1
, we have

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}^{2}\left((T^{\mathrm{h}}_{u}\partial_{x}G)_{\Phi}-T^{\mathrm{h}}_{u}\partial_{x}G_{\Phi}\right)\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\partial_{y}^{2}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{3}{2}+})}\|G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}+C\|\partial_{y}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{3}{2}+})}\|\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+C\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{3}{2}+})}\|\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{4}}\|G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}.

Noticing (
3.15
) and (
8.5
), we can similarly obtain

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}^{2}\left((T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}G}v)_{\Phi}-T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}G}v_{\Phi}\right)\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{4}}\|G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}.

By using lemma
2.8
, from a similar derivation it yields

\displaystyle\|\frac{1}{2\langle t\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}\left((T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}(y\psi)}v)_{\Phi}-T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}(y\psi)}v_{\Phi}\right)\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{4}}\|G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}.

On the other hand,

\displaystyle\partial_{y}^{2}\left(\frac{y}{\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}\left((T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}v)_{\Phi}-T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}v_{\Phi}\right)dz\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{\langle t\rangle}\left((T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}v)_{\Phi}-T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}v_{\Phi}\right)-\frac{y}{\langle t\rangle}\partial_{y}\left((T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}v)_{\Phi}-T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}v_{\Phi}\right)

It follows from lemma
2.7
that

(10.3)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\|\frac{1}{\langle t\rangle}f\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}\leq\|\frac{1}{\langle t\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}}\partial_{y}f\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}\leq\|\partial_{y}^{2}f\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}},\\
&~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\|\frac{y}{\langle t\rangle}f\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}\leq\|\partial_{y}f\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}.\end{aligned}

By (
10.3
), from a similar derivation we have

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}^{2}\left(\frac{y}{\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}\left((T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}v)_{\Phi}-T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}v_{\Phi}\right)dz\right)\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{4}}\|G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}.

Notice that if
\lim_{y\rightarrow+\infty}f=0
, then
f=-\int_{y}^{\infty}\partial_{y}fdz
. Using (
4.3
), we can get

(10.4)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v},\Psi}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{s})}\leq C\langle t\rangle^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}f\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}.\end{aligned}

Then by using lemma
2.2
, lemma
2.8
, (
3.15
) and
(
10.4
), we can obtain

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}^{2}\left((T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{x}G}u)_{\Phi}+R^{\mathrm{h}}(u,\partial_{x}G)_{\Phi}\right)\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\partial_{y}^{2}G\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v},\frac{1}{2}\Psi}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{3}{2}+})}\|u_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\frac{1}{2}\Psi}^{s,0}}+C\|\partial_{y}G\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v},\frac{1}{2}\Psi}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{3}{2}+})}\|\partial_{y}u_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\frac{1}{2}\Psi}^{s,0}}

\displaystyle+C\|G\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v},\frac{1}{2}\Psi}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{3}{2}+})}\|\partial_{y}^{2}u_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\frac{1}{2}\Psi}^{s,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\langle t\rangle^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{3}G\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{3}{2}+,0}}\|G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}+C\langle t\rangle^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}G\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{3}{2}+,0}}\|\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}

\displaystyle+C\langle t\rangle^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}G\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{3}{2}+,0}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{4}}\|G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}.

Similarly , by (
8.5
) we deduce

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}^{2}\left((T^{\mathrm{h}}_{v}\partial_{y}G)_{\Phi}+R^{\mathrm{h}}(v,\partial_{y}G)_{\Phi}\right)\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\partial_{y}^{2}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v},\frac{1}{2}\Psi}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\frac{1}{2}\Psi}^{s,0}}+C\|\partial_{y}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v},\frac{1}{2}\Psi}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\frac{1}{2}\Psi}^{s,0}}

\displaystyle+C\|v\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v},\frac{1}{2}\Psi}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\frac{1}{2}\Psi}^{s,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\langle t\rangle^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}G\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{3}{2}+,0}}\|\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}+C\langle t\rangle^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}G\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{3}{2}+,0}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}

\displaystyle+C\langle t\rangle^{\frac{1}{4}}\|G\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{3}{2}+,0}}\|\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}.

Finally, using lemma
2.8
and (
10.3
), combined with the above estimates we can obtain

\displaystyle\|\frac{1}{2\langle t\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}\left((T^{\mathrm{h}}_{v}\partial_{y}(y\psi))_{\Phi}+R^{\mathrm{h}}(v,\partial_{y}(y\psi))_{\Phi}\right)\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}.

And

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}^{2}\left(\frac{y}{\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}\left((T^{\mathrm{h}}_{v}\partial_{y}u)_{\Phi}+R^{\mathrm{h}}(v,\partial_{y}u)_{\Phi}\right)dz\right)\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+\frac{2}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}.

By combining all the above estimates, we have completed the proof of lemma
10.1
.
∎

Next is the estimates of
(\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})
, and the proposition is shown below:

Proposition 10.1
.

Let
\kappa\in(0,2)
be a given constant. Let
(G,\tilde{G})
be defined by (
2.5
), and
a(t)
be a non-negative and non-decreasing function on
\mathbb{R}_{+}
. Then when
\alpha\leq\gamma_{0}+\frac{5}{36}
, the following inequality holds for any
t<T_{*}
and sufficiently small
\eta>0
.

(10.5)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}(t),\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(t))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}-\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a^{\prime}}(\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}ds\\
&+2\int_{0}^{t}\langle s\rangle^{-1}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}ds+(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{3}{25}\eta-C\epsilon)\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}ds\\
&+2\left(\lambda-C(\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}})\right)\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}ds\\
\leq&\|\sqrt{a}(0)(\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}(0),\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(0))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+C(\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}})\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}(G_{\Phi},\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}ds\\
&+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}ds+C\epsilon\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}ds,\end{aligned}

where
l_{\kappa}=\frac{\kappa(2-\kappa)}{4}\in(0,\frac{1}{4}].

Proof.

By taking
H_{\Psi}^{3,0}
inner product of (
10.1
) with, and using (
4.4
), we deduce

(10.6)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \frac{1}{2}&\frac{d}{dt}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}-\frac{1}{2}\|\sqrt{a^{\prime}}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+\langle t\rangle^{-1}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\\
&+\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}+\frac{1}{2}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\\
\leq&a\left|(\partial_{y}^{2}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{u}\partial_{x}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|+a\left|(\partial_{y}^{2}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}G}v_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|\\
&+\frac{a}{2\langle t\rangle}\left|(\partial_{y}^{2}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}(y\psi)}v_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|+a\left|\left(\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{y}{\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}v_{\Phi}dz),\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|\\
&+a\dot{\theta}\left|(\partial_{y}^{2}P_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|+a\dot{\theta}\left|\left(\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{y}{2\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}P_{\Phi}dz),\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\right)_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|\\
&+a\left|(\partial_{y}^{2}Z,\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|\\
=&\sum_{i=1}^{7}R_{i}.\end{aligned}

For
R_{1}
, notice

\displaystyle\partial_{y}^{2}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{u}\partial_{x}G_{\Phi}=T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}^{2}u}\partial_{x}G_{\Phi}+2T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}\partial_{y}\partial_{x}G_{\Phi}+T^{\mathrm{h}}_{u}\partial_{x}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}.

By integrating by parts, lemma
2.2
, lemma
2.4
, lemma

4.1
and (
8.9
), we have

\displaystyle R_{1}\leq

\displaystyle Ca\left|(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}^{2}u}\partial_{x}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|+Ca\left|(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}\partial_{y}\partial_{x}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|

\displaystyle+Ca\left|(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{u}\partial_{x}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Ca\left|(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}^{2}u}\partial_{x}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|+Ca\left|(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}\partial_{x}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|

\displaystyle+Ca\left|(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}\partial_{x}G_{\Phi},\frac{y}{2\langle t\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|+Ca\left|(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{u}\partial_{x}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\partial_{y}^{2}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+C\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{3}{2}+})}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}

\displaystyle+C\|\partial_{y}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}

\displaystyle+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-2\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}})\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}},

here we use
\alpha\leq\gamma_{0}+\frac{5}{36}
and
\gamma_{0}>1.

For
R_{2}
, notice

\displaystyle\partial_{y}^{2}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}G}v_{\Phi}=T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}^{3}G}v_{\Phi}+2T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}^{2}G}\partial_{y}v_{\Phi}+T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}G}\partial_{y}^{2}v_{\Phi}.

By integrating by parts, we can deduce

\displaystyle R_{2}=

\displaystyle a\left|(T_{\partial_{y}^{3}G}v_{\Phi}+T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}^{2}G}\partial_{y}v_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}-(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}G}\partial_{y}v_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}+\frac{y}{2\langle t\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right|.

Next applying lemma
2.2
, (
3.15
) and (
8.5
), we can derive

\displaystyle R_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\left(C\|\partial_{y}^{3}G\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{1}{2}+,0}}\|\sqrt{a}v_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{3})}+C\|\partial_{y}^{2}G\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{1}{2}+,0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}v_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{3})}\right)\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}

\displaystyle+C\|\partial_{y}G\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{1}{2}+,0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}v_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{3})}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-2\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{3}{4}}\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{3}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}.

Using the fact
\alpha\leq\gamma_{0}+\frac{5}{36}
and
\gamma_{0}>1
, we can deduce

\displaystyle R_{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}+C\epsilon\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}.

For
R_{3},
using lemma
2.8
, (
3.15
) and the estimates of
R_{2}
, it gives

\displaystyle R_{3}\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}+C\epsilon\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}.

For
R_{4},
noticing

\displaystyle\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{y}{\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}v_{\Phi}dz)=-\frac{2}{\langle t\rangle}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}v_{\Phi}-\frac{y}{\langle t\rangle}\partial_{y}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}v_{\Phi},

and combining (
8.9
), then we can use a similar derivation of
R_{2}
to get the estimates of
R_{4}
.

\displaystyle R_{4}\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}+C\epsilon\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}},

For the sake of brevity, no detailed calculation is given here.

For
R_{5}
, by (
2.4
) we have

\displaystyle\dot{\theta}\partial_{y}P_{\Phi}=\partial_{y}(b\partial_{x}b+h\partial_{y}b)_{\Phi}=(b\partial_{y}\partial_{x}b+h\partial_{y}^{2}b)_{\Phi}.

Applying (
2.20
), lemma
2.2
, lemma
2.8
and lemma

4.1
, we can deduce

(10.7)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \dot{\theta}\|\partial_{y}P_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}\leq&\|b_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v},\frac{1}{2}\Psi}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|\partial_{y}b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\frac{1}{2}\Psi}^{s+1,0}}+\|\partial_{y}b_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v},\frac{1}{2}\Psi}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{3}{2}+})}\|b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\frac{1}{2}\Psi}^{s,0}}\\
&+\|h_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v},\frac{1}{2}\Psi}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|\partial_{y}^{2}b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\frac{1}{2}\Psi}^{s,0}}+\|\partial_{y}^{2}b_{\Phi}\|_{L^{2}_{\mathrm{v},\frac{1}{2}\Psi}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v},\frac{1}{2}\Psi}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{s})}\\
\leq&C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}\\
&+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}\\
\leq&C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}\\
&+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}.\end{aligned}

Thus by integrating by parts, (
8.9
) and
(
10.7
), it’s easy to get

\displaystyle R_{5}\leq

\displaystyle a\dot{\theta}\|\partial_{y}P_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\|\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{3}{4}}\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{3}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}

\displaystyle+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-2\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}

\displaystyle+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\langle t\rangle^{\alpha-2\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}.

Using the fact that
\alpha\leq\gamma_{0}+\frac{5}{36}
and
\gamma_{0}>1
, we have

\displaystyle R_{5}\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}+(C\epsilon+\frac{1}{50}\eta)\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}.

For
R_{6},
notice that

\displaystyle\partial_{y}(\frac{y}{\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}P_{\Phi}dz)=\frac{1}{\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}P_{\Phi}dz-\frac{y}{\langle t\rangle}P_{\Phi}.

Then by using (
10.3
) and the estimates of
R_{5}
, we can know that
R_{6}
satisfies

\displaystyle R_{6}\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}+(C\epsilon+\frac{1}{50}\eta)\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}.

For
R_{7}
, applying lemma
10.1
, we have

\displaystyle R_{7}\leq

\displaystyle C\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}Z\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{8}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{8}{3},0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+C(\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}})\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}},

here we use
\alpha\leq\gamma_{0}+\frac{5}{36}
and
\gamma_{0}>1.

Taking
R_{1}-R_{7}
into (
10.6
), and integrating over
[0,t]
, we can deduce the estimates of
\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}
.

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}(t)\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}-\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a^{\prime}}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}ds+2\int_{0}^{t}\langle s\rangle^{-1}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}ds

\displaystyle+2\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}ds+\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{a}(0)\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}(0)\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+C(\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}})\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle+(C\epsilon+\frac{3}{25}\eta)\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}ds+C(\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}})\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}(G_{\Phi},\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}ds+C\epsilon\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}ds.

This completes the estimates of
\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}
. And the estimates of
\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}
can be obtained in the same way, so we have completed the proof of proposition
10.1
.

∎

Using proposition
10.1
, we can prove proposition
3.7
.

Proof of Proposition
3.7
:

Taking
a(t)=\langle t\rangle^{4+2l_{\kappa}-2\eta}
in (
10.5
), then we have

\displaystyle\|\langle t\rangle^{2+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}(t),\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(t))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{3}{25}\eta-C\epsilon)\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{2+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}ds

\displaystyle+2\left(\lambda-C(\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}})\right)\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{2+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|(\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}(0),\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(0))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+C(\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}})\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{1+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(G_{\Phi},\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle+C\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}ds+C\epsilon\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{1+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}ds

\displaystyle+(2+2l_{\kappa}-2\eta)\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}ds.

By (
3.20
) and (
3.21
), we can deduce

\displaystyle\|\langle t\rangle^{2+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}(t),\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(t))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{3}{25}\eta-C\epsilon)\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{2+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}ds

\displaystyle+2\left(\lambda-C(\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}})\right)\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{2+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{C}{\eta(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{7}{25}\eta)}\left(\|(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}(0),\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(0))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+\|(G_{\Phi}(0),\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(0))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}+\|(\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}(0),\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(0))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\right)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{C}{\eta(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{7}{25}\eta)}\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}\sqrt{a}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}ds.

Thus when
\epsilon<\epsilon_{3}
and
\lambda>\lambda_{2}
, proposition
3.7
holds.

\square

11.
The Gevrey Estimates of
\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}
and
\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}

In this section we come to estimate the Gevrey norm of
(\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})
, while in
[
26
]
only the Sobolev norm is estimated. The readers can see the description in section
10
for the reason.

Acting
\partial_{y}
on (
10.1
), we can obtain

(11.1)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &~~~~~\partial_{t}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}+\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)[D_{x}]^{\frac{2}{3}}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}-\partial_{y}^{5}G_{\Phi}+\langle t\rangle^{-1}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}+\partial_{y}\mathfrak{F}=\partial_{y}^{3}Z,\end{aligned}

where
\mathfrak{F}
is defined as follows

(11.2)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \mathfrak{F}=&\partial_{y}^{2}\left(T^{\mathrm{h}}_{u}\partial_{x}G_{\Phi}+T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}G}v_{\Phi}-\frac{1}{2\langle t\rangle}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}(y\psi)}v_{\Phi}+\frac{y}{\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}v_{\Phi}dz-\dot{\theta}P_{\Phi}\right.\\
&\left.+\frac{y}{2\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}\dot{\theta}P_{\Phi}dz\right).\end{aligned}

By (
10.1
) and (
10.2
), we can know the boundary condition

(11.3)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} (-\partial_{y}^{4}G_{\Phi}+\mathfrak{F}-\partial_{y}^{2}Z)_{y=0}=0.\end{aligned}

First we give the estimates of
\mathfrak{F}
.

Lemma 11.1
.

Let
\mathfrak{F}
be defined by (
11.2
). Then for any
t<T_{*},

\mathfrak{F}
satisfies

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{F}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|(\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|(\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{4}}\|(G_{\Phi},\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}.

Proof.

We estimate them term by term. First of all, by lemma
2.2
and lemma
4.1
, we can know that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}^{2}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{u}\partial_{x}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}\leq

\displaystyle C\|T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}^{2}u}\partial_{x}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}+C\|T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}\partial_{y}\partial_{x}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}+C\|T^{\mathrm{h}}_{u}\partial_{y}^{2}\partial_{x}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\partial_{y}^{2}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}+C\|\partial_{y}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}

\displaystyle+C\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{4}}\|G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}.

By applying lemma
2.2
, (
3.15
) and (
8.5
), we derive that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}^{2}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}G}v_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}\leq

\displaystyle C\|T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}^{3}G}v_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}+C\|T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}^{2}G}\partial_{y}v_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}+C\|T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}G}\partial_{y}^{2}v_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\partial_{y}^{3}G\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{1}{2}+,0}}\|v_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{s})}+C\|\partial_{y}^{2}G\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{1}{2}+,0}}\|\partial_{y}v_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{s})}

\displaystyle+C\|\partial_{y}G\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{1}{2}+,0}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}v_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{s})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{4}}\|G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}.

Using lemma
2.8
and (
10.3
), combined with the above estimates, we can similarly obtain

\displaystyle\|\frac{1}{2\langle t\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}(y\psi)}v_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{4}}\|G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}.

And

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}^{2}\left(\frac{y}{\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}T^{\mathrm{h}}_{\partial_{y}u}v_{\Phi}dz\right)\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{4}}\|G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}.

It follows from (
2.4
) that

\displaystyle\dot{\theta}\partial_{y}^{2}P_{\Phi}=(\partial_{y}b\partial_{x}\partial_{y}b+b\partial_{x}\partial_{y}^{2}b-\partial_{x}b\partial_{y}^{2}b+h\partial_{y}^{3}b)_{\Phi}.

Use (
2.20
) to estimate them term by term,

\displaystyle\|(\partial_{y}b\partial_{x}\partial_{y}b)_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}\leq

\displaystyle C\|\partial_{y}b_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|\partial_{y}b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}+C\|\partial_{y}b_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{3}{2}+})}\|\partial_{y}b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}},

\displaystyle\|(b\partial_{x}\partial_{y}^{2}b)_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}\leq

\displaystyle C\|b_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|\partial_{y}^{2}b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}+C\|\partial_{y}^{2}b_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{3}{2}+})}\|b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{4}}\|\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}},

\displaystyle\|(\partial_{x}b\partial_{y}^{2}b)_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}\leq

\displaystyle C\|b_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{3}{2}+})}\|\partial_{y}^{2}b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}+C\|\partial_{y}^{2}b_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{4}}\|\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}.

Noticing (
8.5
) also holds for
(h_{\Phi},b_{\Phi},\tilde{G}_{\Phi})
, thus we have

\displaystyle\|(h\partial_{y}^{3}b)_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}\leq

\displaystyle C\|h_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{\frac{1}{2}+})}\|\partial_{y}^{3}b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}+C\|\partial_{y}^{3}b_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{1}{2}+,0}}\|h_{\Phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{v}}(H_{\mathrm{h}}^{s})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{4}}\|\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}.

Combining the above estimates, we can deduce

\displaystyle\dot{\theta}\|\partial_{y}^{2}P_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{4}}\|\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}.

Finally using (
10.3
), it’s similar to yield

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}^{2}(\frac{y}{2\langle t\rangle}\int_{y}^{\infty}\dot{\theta}P_{\Phi}dz)\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s,0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{4}}\|\tilde{G}_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{s+1,0}}.

Combining all the above estimates, we complete the proof of lemma
11.1
.
∎

With lemma
11.1
, we can now estimate
(\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})
.

Proposition 11.1
.

Let
\kappa\in(0,2)
be a given constant. Let
(G,\tilde{G})
be defined by (
2.5
), and
a(t)
be a non-negative and non-decreasing function on
\mathbb{R}_{+}
. Then when
\alpha\leq\gamma_{0}+\frac{5}{36}
, the following inequality holds for any
t<T_{*}
and sufficiently small
\eta>0
.

(11.4)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}(t),\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(t))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}-\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a^{\prime}}(\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}ds\\
&+2\int_{0}^{t}\langle s\rangle^{-1}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}ds+(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{1}{25}\eta)\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}^{4}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{4}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}ds\\
&+2(\lambda-C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}})\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{7}{3},0}}ds\\
\leq&\|\sqrt{a}(0)(\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}(0),\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(0))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{-\frac{3}{2}}\sqrt{a}(G_{\Phi},\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}ds\\
&+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}ds\\
&+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}ds,\end{aligned}

where
l_{\kappa}=\frac{\kappa(2-\kappa)}{4}\in(0,\frac{1}{4}].

Proof.

Taking
H_{\Psi}^{2,0}
inner product of (
11.1
) with
a(t)\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}
, noticing the boundary condition (
11.3
), using (
4.4
) and integrating by parts we can get that

(11.5)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \frac{1}{2}&\frac{d}{dt}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}-\frac{1}{2}\|\sqrt{a^{\prime}}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}+\langle t\rangle^{-1}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}\\
&+\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{7}{3},0}}+\frac{1}{2}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{4}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}\\
\leq&a\left|(\mathfrak{F}-\partial_{y}^{2}Z,\partial_{y}^{4}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}\right|+a\left|(\mathfrak{F}-\partial_{y}^{2}Z,\frac{y}{2\langle t\rangle}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}\right|.\end{aligned}

From lemma
10.1
and lemma
11.1
we can find

\displaystyle a\left|(\mathfrak{F}-\partial_{y}^{2}Z,\partial_{y}^{4}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{4}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{4}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{3}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{4}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\langle t\rangle^{-\gamma_{0}-\frac{5}{4}}\|\sqrt{a}(G_{\Phi},\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{4}G_{\Phi}\|_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{3}{2}}\sqrt{a}(G_{\Phi},\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{7}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{4}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}},

here we use the fact that
\alpha\leq\gamma_{0}+\frac{5}{36}
and
\gamma_{0}>1.

Next by (
10.3
) and the above estimates, we can deduce

\displaystyle a\left|(\mathfrak{F}-\partial_{y}^{2}Z,\frac{y}{2\langle t\rangle}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi})_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{3}{2}}\sqrt{a}(G_{\Phi},\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{7}{3},0}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{100}\eta\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{4}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}.

Taking all the above estimates into (
11.5
), and integrating over
[0,t]
, we can know that
\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}
satisfies

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}(t)\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}-\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a^{\prime}}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}ds+2\int_{0}^{t}\langle s\rangle^{-1}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}ds

\displaystyle+2\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{7}{3},0}}ds+\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{4}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{a}(0)\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}(0)\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{7}{3},0}}ds+\frac{1}{25}\eta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{a}\partial_{y}^{4}G_{\Phi}\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}ds

\displaystyle+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{-\frac{3}{2}}\sqrt{a}(G_{\Phi},\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}ds+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{-1}\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{a}(\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}ds.

This completes the estimates of
\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}
. And the estimates of
\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}
can be obtained in the same way, so we have completed the proof of proposition
11.1
.
∎

Finally we can use proposition
11.1
to prove proposition
3.8
.

Proof of Proposition
3.8
:

Taking
a(t)=\langle t\rangle^{5+2l_{\kappa}-2\eta}
in (
11.4
), then we have

\displaystyle\|\langle t\rangle^{\frac{5}{2}+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}(t),\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(t))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}+(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{1}{25}\eta)\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{\frac{5}{2}+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}^{4}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{4}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}ds

\displaystyle+2(\lambda-C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}})\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{\frac{5}{2}+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{7}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|(\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}(0),\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(0))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{1+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(G_{\Phi},\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{10}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle+C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{2+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{13}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle+(3+2l_{\kappa}-2\eta)\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{2+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}ds.

By applying (
3.20
), (
3.21
) and
(
3.22
) we deduce

\displaystyle\|\langle t\rangle^{\frac{5}{2}+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}(t),\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(t))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}+(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{1}{25}\eta)\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle s\rangle^{\frac{5}{2}+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}^{4}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{4}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}ds

\displaystyle+2(\lambda-C\eta^{-1}\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}})\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{\frac{5}{2}+l_{\kappa}-\eta}(\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi},\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{G}_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{7}{3},0}}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{C}{\eta(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{7}{25}\eta)(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{13}{100}\eta)}\left(\|(\partial_{y}G_{\Phi}(0),\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(0))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+\|(G_{\Phi}(0),\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(0))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{4,0}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\|(\partial_{y}^{2}G_{\Phi}(0),\partial_{y}^{2}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(0))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{3,0}}+\|(\partial_{y}^{3}G_{\Phi}(0),\partial_{y}^{3}\tilde{G}_{\Phi}(0))\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{2,0}}\right)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{C}{\eta(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{7}{25}\eta)(4l_{\kappa}-\frac{13}{100}\eta)}\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}\|\langle s\rangle^{l_{\kappa}-\eta}\sqrt{a}(u_{\Phi},b_{\Phi})\|^{2}_{H_{\Psi}^{\frac{22}{3},0}}ds.

Thus when
\epsilon<\epsilon_{3}
and
\lambda>\lambda_{2}
, proposition
3.8
holds.

\square
